- **agent-s-mock-drifted-from-the-real-api-1a2d7db47cc5bdb5**: Detects test mocks that have drifted from the real API's shape or behavior by diffing mocks against the current schema on a schedule. - **agent-s-monorepo-bump-updated-the-root-ceec351ebd0932ae**: Fixes monorepo bumps that updated the root lockfile but left nested workspace lockfiles stale, so sibling packages install old versions. - **agent-s-monorepo-bump-updated-the-shar-36aa07c7ffdccb77**: Propagates a monorepo shared-library bump to the apps that pin it. Use it when the library moved but apps still install the old version. Key trigger: the app lockfiles or manifests pin the library below the new version. Not for: apps that intentionally pin an older library version, which is a policy choice. - **agent-s-mtls-client-certificate-expire-cadb7a26c08560f2**: A step-by-step skill for fixing pipelines broken by an expired mTLS client certificate: confirming expiry with openssl, renewing, automating renewal, and adding expiry alerting. - **agent-s-multi-threaded-verify-broke-th-e874e3ffb13318fd**: Fixes agent multi-threaded verify breaking a shared manifest: serialise manifest writes, use per-thread outputs with a merge step, or move manifest state to a store with transactions. - **agent-s-n-1-alert-fired-on-a-nightly-b-0f9125fdd45b8552**: Teaches a profiler agent to distinguish bulk background jobs from hot request paths so N+1 detectors stop firing on nightly imports. - **agent-s-nbconvert-timed-out-on-a-noteb-62abefda4dd3bab5**: Fixes an agent's nbconvert timing out on a notebook with 300 executed cells: the outputs make the file huge and execution slow. - **agent-s-nbformat-write-failed-lost-the-5226c541dd96f757**: Fixes an agent's nbformat write dropping the cell tags papermill needs: preserve tags on every cell transformation. For agent builders. Use when a long monitoring task dies on auth. Not for a credential that was revoked for security reasons, which needs a human decision. - **agent-s-oauth-token-refresh-looped-unt-cd13380e5c49356c**: A step-by-step skill for fixing an agent's OAuth refresh loop that gets the identity provider to rate-limit it: caching the token, single-flight refresh, and exponential backoff with jitter.
